self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT 1 FROM campaigns WHERE id = {};\",\n            sql_text(&campaign.id)\n        ))?;\n        if !exists.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(false);\n        }\n        self.sql(&format!(\n            \"INSERT INTO campaigns(id,name,dm) VALUES ({},{},{});\",\n            sql_text(&campaign.id),\n            sql_text(&campaign.name),\n            sql_text(&campaign.dm)\n        ))\n        .map(|_| true)\n    }\n\n    fn create_play_campaign(&self, campaign: &PlayCampaign) -> Result<bool, ()> {\n        let exists = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaigns WHERE id = {};\",\n            sql_text(&campaign.id)\n        ))?;\n        if !exists.trim().is_empty() {\n            PlayReplayResult::Forbidden => return Ok(PlayReplayResult::Forbidden),\n            PlayReplayResult::Conflict => return Err(()),\n        }\n        if !self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_replay_events WHERE campaign_id = {} AND event_id = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(event_id)))?.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayReplayResult::Conflict);\n        }\n        let sequence: i64 = self.sql(&format!(\"BEGIN IMMEDIATE; INSERT INTO play_campaign_replay_events(campaign_id,sequence,event_id,text) VALUES ({},COALESCE((SELECT MAX(sequence) + 1 FROM play_campaign_replay_events WHERE campaign_id = {}),1),{},{}); SELECT MAX(sequence) FROM play_campaign_replay_events WHERE campaign_id = {}; COMMIT;\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(event_id), sql_text(text), sql_text(campaign_id)))?.trim().parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\n        Ok(PlayReplayResult::Found(PlayReplayEvent { event_id: event_id.to_string(), text: text.to_string(), sequence }))\n    }\n\n    fn play_replay(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str) -> Result<PlayReplayResult<Vec<PlayReplayEvent>>, ()> {\n        match self.play_replay_access(campaign_id, actor)? {\n            PlayReplayResult::Found(()) => {}\n            PlayReplayResult::UnknownCampaign => return Ok(PlayReplayResult::UnknownCampaign),\n            PlayReplayResult::Forbidden => return Ok(PlayReplayResult::Forbidden),\n            PlayReplayResult::Conflict => return Err(()),\n        }\n        let rows = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT sequence,hex(event_id),hex(text) FROM play_campaign_replay_events WHERE campaign_id = {} ORDER BY sequence;\", sql_text(campaign_id)))?;\n        let mut events = Vec::new();\n        for row in rows.lines() {\n            let mut fields = row.split('|');\n            let event = PlayReplayEvent { sequence: fields.next().ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())?, event_id: decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?, text: decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)? };\n            if fields.next().is_some() { return Err(()); }\n            events.push(event);\n        }\n        Ok(PlayReplayResult::Found(events))\n    }\n\n    fn play_rng_access(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str) -> Result<PlayRngResult<bool>, ()> {\n        let owner = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT hex(owner) FROM play_campaigns WHERE id = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id)))?;\n        let owner = owner.trim();\n        if owner.is_empty() { return Ok(PlayRngResult::UnknownCampaign); }\n        let is_owner = decode_sql_text(owner)? == actor;\n        if is_owner || !self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_members WHERE campaign_id = {} AND username = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(actor)))?.trim().is_empty() {\n            Ok(PlayRngResult::Found(is_owner))\n        } else {\n            Ok(PlayRngResult::Forbidden)\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn configure_play_rng_seed(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, seed: &str) -> Result<PlayRngResult<()>, ()> {\n        match self.play_rng_access(campaign_id, actor)? {\n            PlayRngResult::Found(true) => {}\n            PlayRngResult::Found(false) => return Ok(PlayRngResult::Forbidden),\n            PlayRngResult::UnknownCampaign => return Ok(PlayRngResult::UnknownCampaign),\n            PlayRngResult::Forbidden => return Ok(PlayRngResult::Forbidden),\n            _ => return Err(()),\n        }\n        if !self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_rng_seeds WHERE campaign_id = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id)))?.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayRngResult::Conflict);\n        }\n        self.sql(&format!(\"INSERT INTO play_campaign_rng_seeds(campaign_id,seed) VALUES ({},{});\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(seed)))?;\n        Ok(PlayRngResult::Found(()))\n    }\n\n    fn append_play_rng_roll(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, roll_id: &str, sides: i64) -> Result<PlayRngResult<PlayRngRoll>, ()> {\n        match self.play_rng_access(campaign_id, actor)? {\n            PlayRngResult::Found(_) => {}\n            PlayRngResult::UnknownCampaign => return Ok(PlayRngResult::UnknownCampaign),\n            PlayRngResult::Forbidden => return Ok(PlayRngResult::Forbidden),\n            _ => return Err(()),\n        }\n        let seed = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT hex(seed) FROM play_campaign_rng_seeds WHERE campaign_id = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id)))?;\n        let seed = seed.trim();\n        if seed.is_empty() { return Ok(PlayRngResult::MissingSeed); }\n        if !self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_rng_rolls WHERE campaign_id = {} AND roll_id = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(roll_id)))?.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayRngResult::Conflict);\n        }\n        let seed = decode_sql_text(seed)?;\n        let sequence: i64 = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT COALESCE(MAX(sequence) + 1, 1) FROM play_campaign_rng_rolls WHERE campaign_id = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id)))?.trim().parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\n        let mut acc = 0u32;\n        for byte in format!(\"{}|{}|{}|{}\", seed, sequence, roll_id, sides).bytes() {\n            acc = acc.wrapping_mul(31).wrapping_add(u32::from(byte));\n        }\n        let result = i64::from(acc % sides as u32 + 1);\n        self.sql(&format!(\"INSERT INTO play_campaign_rng_rolls(campaign_id,sequence,roll_id,sides,result) VALUES ({},{},{},{},{});\", sql_text(campaign_id), sequence, sql_text(roll_id), sides, result))?;\n        Ok(PlayRngResult::Found(PlayRngRoll { roll_id: roll_id.to_string(), sides, result, sequence }))\n    }\n\n    fn play_rng_ledger(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str) -> Result<PlayRngResult<(String, Vec<PlayRngRoll>)>, ()> {\n        match self.play_rng_access(campaign_id, actor)? {\n            PlayRngResult::Found(_) => {}\n            PlayRngResult::UnknownCampaign => return Ok(PlayRngResult::UnknownCampaign),\n            PlayRngResult::Forbidden => return Ok(PlayRngResult::Forbidden),\n            _ => return Err(()),\n        }\n        let seed = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT hex(seed) FROM play_campaign_rng_seeds WHERE campaign_id = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id)))?;\n        let seed = seed.trim();\n        if seed.is_empty() { return Ok(PlayRngResult::MissingSeed); }\n        let rows = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT sequence,hex(roll_id),sides,result FROM play_campaign_rng_rolls WHERE campaign_id = {} ORDER BY sequence;\", sql_text(campaign_id)))?;\n        let mut rolls = Vec::new();\n        for row in rows.lines() {\n            let mut fields = row.split('|');\n            let roll = PlayRngRoll { sequence: fields.next().ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())?, roll_id: decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?, sides: fields.next().ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())?, result: fields.next().ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())? };\n            if fields.next().is_some() { return Err(()); }\n            rolls.push(roll);\n        }\n        Ok(PlayRngResult::Found((decode_sql_text(seed)?, rolls)))\n    }\n\n    fn create_play_idempotent_event(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, event_id: &str, value: &str, idempotency_key: &str) -> Result<PlayIdempotentResult<PlayIdempotentEvent>, ()> {\n        let Some(is_owner) = self.play_projection_access(campaign_id, actor)? else { return Ok(PlayIdempotentResult::UnknownCampaign); };\n        if !is_owner && self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_members WHERE campaign_id = {} AND username = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(actor)))?.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayIdempotentResult::Forbidden);\n        }\n        let rows = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT hex(event_id),hex(value),sequence,hex(idempotency_key) FROM play_campaign_idempotent_events WHERE campaign_id = {} AND idempotency_key = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(idempotency_key)))?;\n        if !rows.trim().is_empty() {\n            let mut fields = rows.trim().split('|');\n            let stored_event_id = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\n            let stored_value = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\n            let sequence = fields.next().ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\nfn handle(\n    stream: &mut TcpStream,\n    sessions: &mut Sessions,\n    users: &mut Users,\n    storage: &mut Storage,\n    maintenance_mode: idempotency_key,\n    }) = request\n    else {\n        return respond(stream, 400, r#\"{\"error\":\"bad request\"}\"#);\n    };\n    if method == \"GET\" && path == \"/healthz\" {\n        return respond(stream, 200, r#\"{\"status\":\"ok\"}\"#);\n    }\n    if method == \"GET\" && path == \"/readyz\" {\n        return if *maintenance_mode {\n            respond(stream, 503, r#\"{\"status\":\"maintenance\",\"schema_version\":2}\"#)\n        } else {\n            respond(stream, 200, r#\"{\"status\":\"ready\",\"schema_version\":2}\"#)\n        };\n    }\n    if path.starts_with(\"/v1/play/campaigns/\") {\n        let delegation_path = path.strip_prefix(\"/v1/play/campaigns/\");\n        if method == \"POST\" {\n            if let Some(campaign_id) =
